- **sabotage-verifier**: Proves a test can actually FAIL. Breaks the code under test on purpose with a precise inverse edit, runs only the affected assembly, reports WHICH test reddened, and restores. ([PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage](https://github.com/PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sabotage-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **saboteur**: Makes a task's checks pass without doing the work, in a disposable worktree, to find the holes an implementing agent could fall through. Given the task, the oracle command, and the passing condition, it tries every cheat an implementer could reach and reports each one that worked with its diff and the oracle's output, plus the ones that were blocked. Use after writing checks that will gate unattended agent work, and again after patching. Skip it when a person reviews every diff anyway or the checks gate a one-off task. For flaws in the code rather than the checks, use repertoire:antagonist. ([stevekinney/repertoire](https://github.com/stevekinney/repertoire/blob/HEAD/agents/saboteur.md) · ★18)
